We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [98] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Puffin-World，一种统一的多模态架构，将物理理解、空间模拟以及3D世界生成与重建集成于一体，无需依赖外部离线模块。该框架联合建模三种原生世界状态（物理/重力场与纬度、几何/深度、外观/图像），并引入统一的Omni-Camera表示以支持多样任务和灵活运动。通过在真实世界中锚定绝对相机属性，实现物理一致且视觉稳定的世界生成，并将外观与几何耦合在单一生成过程中，支持闭环交叉任务应用。同时构建包含1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组和100万轨迹的Puffin-16M数据集以支持复杂场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D世界生成与理解方法通常依赖多个外部离线模块（如单独的视频生成器、深度估计器和物理模拟器），缺乏统一的架构来同时处理物理理解、空间模拟和3D世界生成与重建，导致跨任务协同困难、系统复杂且难以实现闭环应用。

Method: Puffin-World统一建模三种原生世界状态：物理状态（重力场和纬度）、几何状态（深度）和外观状态（图像），并通过统一的Omni-Camera表示支持多样任务与灵活运动。框架提出跨未来帧的物理动力学传播策略，将绝对相机属性锚定于真实世界以获得物理一致性。同时将外观与几何耦合于单一生成过程，联合合成未来视角并重建其底层几何结构。配套构建了Puffin-16M数据集（1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组及100万条轨迹）。

Result: Puffin-World实现了无需外部离线模块的统一多模态架构，能够支持物理一致、视觉稳定的世界生成，以及外观与几何的联合合成与重建。该框架实现模拟与自校准世界探索等交织的闭环应用，并通过Puffin-16M大规模数据集进行了扩展。代码、模型和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: Puffin-World通过将物理、几何和外观表示为统一的世界状态并耦合在一个生成过程中，证明了统一的多模态架构能够有效实现物理一致的3D世界生成和重建，同时支撑需要多任务协同的闭环应用。此类发布开源资源将进一步促进该领域研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Principia基准，通过同一场景中两个物体之间的运动关系一致性来评估视频模型的牛顿物理推理能力，这种评估方法独立于帧率、物体尺度和相机校准。在八个物理现象上测试六个SOTA视频生成器，所有模型得分不超过0.42，尽管它们在VBench上均约0.8。视觉语言模型检测关系物理违规的最佳准确率仅67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型中的物理推理评估困难，因为绝对运动测量依赖于帧率、物体尺度和相机校准等因素，而这些在生成视频中往往不明确或不可用。作者提出利用同一场景中物体运动之间的可预测关系来进行评估，这种关系独立于校准参数，从而克服了现有评估方法的根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出Principia基准，通过两个物体在同一场景中遵守相同物理定律时运动必须满足的可预测关系来评估牛顿物理。基准覆盖八种现象（重力、回弹、摩擦、旋转惯量、抛体运动、动量、摆、质量-弹簧振动），涵盖平移、旋转、碰撞和振荡动力学，使用受控协议录制的真实场景。引入与校准无关的一致性分数，直接在图像空间中量化物理违规。对六个SOTA视频生成器和视觉语言模型进行系统评估。

Result: 六个SOTA视频生成器在Principia上均未超过0.42分，尽管它们在VBench上评分均约0.8，表明这些模型物理一致性严重不足。视觉语言模型检测关系物理违规的表现也有限，最佳模型仅达67%准确率，大多数模型接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有视频生成模型尽管在传统基准（如VBench）上表现良好，但在物理推理方面存在严重缺陷。Principia提供了一种校准无关的物理推理评估方法，揭示当前模型在基础牛顿物理关系层面存在系统性不足，为未来视频生成模型的物理一致性改进提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [100] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Scal3R，一种用于长视频在线 3D 重建的新方法。该方法观察到在线重建模型性能下降的根本原因是相对固定首帧锚点的位姿回归导致外推超过训练分布，虽然全局位姿头失效，但逐帧深度（局部几何）保持稳定。基于这种解耦特性，Scal3R 将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询问题，通过仅占约 1% 参数的轻量可学习 token 注入完全冻结的骨干网络（使用非对称注意力），相对于多个历史关键帧查询位姿，并配合带闭环的在线位姿图优化抑制长程漂移。Scal3R 在单块 GPU 上 8 小时即可收敛，在 KITTI 上比在线基线平均 ATE 降低超过 60%，并在多个数据集上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3D 重建模型在长视频上表现不佳，因为基于固定首帧锚点的相对位姿回归迫使模型在训练分布之外进行外推，微小漂移逐步累积并放大为显著的几何坍塌。作者希望通过揭示"深度稳定而位姿失效"的解耦特性，设计一种能抑制长程漂移的方法。

Method: Scal3R 将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询问题：(1) 使用约 1% 参数的轻量可学习 token，通过非对称注意力注入完全冻结的骨干网络，从而查询相对多个历史关键帧的位姿；(2) 引入带闭环的在线位姿图优化系统，以抑制长程漂移并维持全局一致性。

Result: Scal3R 在单块 GPU 上 8 小时内收敛；在 KITTI 数据集上平均 ATE 比在线基线降低超过 60%；在 Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet 和 7-Scenes 等多个基准上实现 SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过解耦"局部几何保持稳定、全局位姿易失效"这一特性，Scal3R 以轻量可学习 token 配合冻结骨干及在线位姿图优化，成功实现了长视频在线 3D 重建中的准确与高效，显著抑制了误差漂移。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenMatch是一种基于Transformer的统一模型，用于估计3D形状对应关系，通过曲率引导的自适应网格token化，在部分和完整形状匹配上均达到领先性能，且推理速度亚秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的3D形状对应估计方法在部分观测和强非等距变形下仍面临挑战。基于学习的方法多依赖手工特征或模板表示，而基于函数映射的生成模型推理成本高、可解释性有限且泛化能力差。需要一种更高效且鲁棒的统一模型来应对部分到部分以及完整形状的匹配任务。

Method: TokenMatch提出一个前馈式Transformer架构，仅在一个挑战性的非等距部分到部分形状匹配数据集BeCoS上训练，即可泛化到完整形状匹配而无需微调。模型利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制学习patch级和点级关系及稠密对应，核心创新在于网格可根据形状曲率引导自适应地token化为patch，从而有效学习形状特定的几何描述子。

Result: 在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等多个标准基准上评估，TokenMatch在平均测地误差和交并比指标上大多优于现有部分和完整形状匹配方法，同时推理速度更快，达到亚秒级。

Conclusion: 实验表明TokenMatch在部分和完整形状匹配上均保持高效且高准确率，验证了曲率引导的token化结合Transformer自/交叉注意力机制的有效性，为3D形状对应估计提供了一种统一且高效的新范式。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S³T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time) 是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪框架。该方法将时间采样密度作为特权信息，利用密集采样视图作为教师、稀疏采样视图作为学生（权重共享）进行知识蒸馏，学生通过匹配教师的下一token分布来学习。无需标签、独立教师或奖励信号，且推理成本为零。在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T将VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（模型融合）、+2.70（结合视觉编码器适配），并能将从无标签合成片段学到的能力迁移至真实视频（VSTAT-YouTube +7.95，MVBench Action Count +4.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如自进化/自蒸馏方法）在连续视频状态追踪任务上效果有限，状态追踪能力几乎未被提升。此外，传统蒸馏需要独立的教师网络或外部标签/奖励信号，成本高昂且不够自包含。作者假设更密集的时间采样视图能够更准确地恢复视频运行状态，并希望利用这一特权信息构建一个无需外部监督即可自我提升的框架。

Method: S³T将时间采样密度视为特权信息：同一视频片段以密集采样（教师视角）和稀疏采样（学生视角）两种视图输入权重共享的同一模型。模型以密集视图的下一token分布作为自生成的目标，稀疏视图的学生通过学习匹配该分布进行自蒸馏。由于目标由模型自身产生，训练无需人工标签、独立教师网络或奖励信号，推理时无需额外开销。方法还支持模型烧汤（souping）集成及视觉编码器适配等扩展。训练数据使用无标签合成视频片段，测试时可迁移至真实视频。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T将VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（结合烧汤技术）、+2.70（额外进行视觉编码器适配）。对比之下，先前的自进化方法在状态追踪上几乎没有提升。无标签合成片段上学到的能力成功迁移至真实视频，在VSTAT-YouTube状态追踪问题上提升+7.95，在MVBench Action Count任务上提升+4.50。

Conclusion: 实验证明，利用时间采样密度作为特权信息进行自蒸馏，能够在不引入任何外部监督的前提下有效提升大视觉语言模型的连续视频状态追踪能力，且学到表征可以从合成域泛化到真实域。S³T为视频理解中的自监督自我进化提供了一个新的范式，同时保持了零推理成本的优势。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Where Does Harness-Optimization Value Live? Localized Gains and the Budget-Splitting Trap in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02889)
*Michael Nguyen,Wei Chen Tan,Nurul Aisyah Hassan,Arvind Raman,Li Hua Lim,Ahmad Faiz Razak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HARNESSEVO，将LLM代理的文本框架（harness）分解为角色、任务策略、工具/格式规则、反思/控制四个可独立进化的槽位，并在相同预算下与扁平字符串进化对比。在ALFWorld（7B冻结骨干）上，整体成功率无显著提升（0.657 vs 0.642/0.642），但槽级分析揭示几乎所有优化价值集中在反思/控制槽（leave-one-in增益+0.119），其他槽位均为无效。均匀预算分配有害（每槽仅16次rollout，低于有效搜索下限导致全部冻结），集中预算于高价值控制槽可将成功率提升至0.761（只需一半预算）。在WebShop上所有槽位冻结为空且方法持平，表明该任务缺乏可言语化的循环控制失败而非预算不足。结论：harness价值是局部化的，均匀预算分配可能有害，信用分配应先于结构化代理进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射式提示进化方法通常将LLM代理的文本框架（harness）作为单一扁平字符串优化，忽视了框架内部各组件（角色、策略、工具规则、控制启发式）对优化价值的贡献差异。作者希望定位优化价值究竟存在于哪个组件，从而指导更高效的代理进化策略。

Method: 将harness分解为四个可独立进化的槽位：role（角色）、task-strategy（任务策略）、tool/format-rules（工具/格式规则）、reflection/control（反思/控制）。在同等预算（iso-budget）条件下，使用相同的反射式优化器，结合leave-one-in和leave-one-out归因方法，量化每个槽位对整体性能的贡献。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，HARNESSEVO整体成功率（0.657）较stock harness（0.642）和扁平进化（0.642）无显著提升；槽级分析显示仅reflection/control槽有价值（leave-one-in增益+0.119），其余槽无效；均匀预算分割导致每槽仅16次rollout低于有效搜索下限，全部冻结于空种子；将预算集中于控制槽后以一半预算达到0.761。在WebShop上所有方法打平且槽位全冻结，表明该任务不存在可言语化的循环控制失败。

Conclusion: harness的优化价值是高度局部化的而非均匀分布；均匀预算分割可能产生主动伤害（低于搜索下限导致完全无效）；在结构化代理进化之前应先进行信用分配，以识别高价值组件并集中优化预算。

Abstract: A growing body of work improves frozen large language models (LLMs) as agents by evolving their harness: the textual scaffolding around the model, including persona, strategy, format rules, and control heuristics. Existing reflective prompt-evolution methods usually optimize this harness as one flat string. We instead ask where the optimization value actually resides. We introduce HARNESSEVO, which decomposes the harness into four separately evolvable slots: role, task-strategy, tool/format-rules, and reflection/control. Using the same reflective optimizer under an iso-budget setting, we pair this decomposition with leave-one-in and leave-one-out attribution to measure the contribution of each slot.
  On ALFWorld with a frozen 7B backbone, HARNESSEVO does not significantly improve the overall binary success rate over either the stock harness or flat-string evolution: 0.657 versus 0.642 and 0.642, respectively. However, the slot-level analysis reveals that nearly all useful optimization value is localized in the reflection/control slot, which achieves a leave-one-in gain of +0.119. The other slots are individually null. We further show that uniform budget splitting is harmful: allocating 64 rollouts across four slots leaves only 16 per slot, below the optimizer's effective search floor, causing every slot to freeze at its empty seed. Concentrating the budget on the high-credit control slot recovers the lost gain, reaching 0.761 with half the split budget.
  The effect is task-contingent. On WebShop, all slots freeze empty and all methods tie, indicating a genuine absence of recurrent, verbalizable control failures rather than budget starvation. Overall, our results suggest that harness value is localized, uniform budget splitting can be actively harmful, and credit assignment should precede structured agent-evolution.

</details>


### [104] [Bounded Personas Match Retrieval on Classification but Not Regression for a Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02890)
*JaeHa Yoon,Minjun Park,Seoyeon Kim,Jiwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Choi,Dohyun Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PersonaLink是一种无需训练的个性化语言智能体方法，它将用户交互历史提炼为紧凑的三字段人设描述，并通过递归自评估与重写机制优化。在LaMP-2新闻分类任务上，该方法与BM25检索方法的准确率在统计上无显著差异，揭示了蒸馏式人设在特定任务上可以匹配检索式方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言智能体需要将用户交互历史转化为对新请求的处理行为。现有两条主流路线是检索（retrieval）与蒸馏（distillation）。检索精确但每查询需支付随历史增长的检索与上下文成本；蒸馏将历史压缩为紧凑的自然语言人设，具有有界、查询无关、可解释的优点，但普遍被认为会牺牲准确率。此前缺乏对蒸馏人设能否匹配检索性能及其适用任务类型的清晰刻画。

Method: PersonaLink是一种训练免费（training-free）方法。它将用户历史蒸馏为有界的三字段人设（bounded three-field persona），并采用递归精炼策略：每一轮在用户自身带标签历史的一个保留切片上对冻结的智能体进行自评估，根据其错误重写人设，且仅当新版本在该切片上没有性能回退时才予以保留。所有比较共享同一个冻结的7B骨干模型，差异仅在于上下文中放置的内容，从而隔离了表示方式与模型本身对性能的影响。

Result: 在LaMP-2（15类新闻分类任务的200位用户）上，PersonaLink达到0.745-0.755的准确率，与BM25检索（0.760-0.765）的差异在统计上不显著，展示了清晰的任务类型不对称性。

Conclusion: 蒸馏式人设在特定任务类型（如新闻分类）上能够匹配检索式方法的准确率，推翻了蒸馏必然牺牲精度的普遍假设。任务类型（而非单纯的方法选择）决定了两者的性能差距。该研究为个性化智能体中表示方式的选取提供了基于任务类型的实证指引。

Abstract: A personalized language agent must convert a user's interaction history into behavior on each new request at inference time. Two strategies dominate. Retrieval pulls a few of the user's most relevant past items into the prompt, which is accurate but pays a per-query selection and context cost that grows with the history. Distillation instead compresses the history once into a compact natural-language persona, which is bounded, query-independent, and interpretable, but is widely assumed to sacrifice accuracy. Whether, and on which tasks, a distilled persona can match retrieval has not been characterized cleanly. We introduce PersonaLink, a training-free method that distills a user's history into a bounded three-field persona and recursively refines it: each pass self-evaluates the frozen agent on a held-out slice of the user's own labeled history, rewrites the persona from its errors, and keeps the result only when it does not regress on that slice. Because every comparison shares one frozen 7B backbone and differs only in what is placed in context, the design isolates the effect of representation from that of the model. The result is a clear task-type asymmetry. On 200 users of LaMP-2 (15-way news categorization), PersonaLink reaches 0.745-0.755 accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from BM25 retrieval (0.760-0.765).

</details>


### [105] [Counterexamples as Feedback for Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02892)
*Sidhesh Badrinarayan,Adithya Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A-CEGIS是一个轻量级框架，使用反例作为反馈来评估多轮自然语言到正则表达式合成中的细粒度修复能力。在30个NL-RX-Turk任务上，诊断性反例反馈在四轮内解决了90%的任务，而零样本生成仅解决17%；完整诊断运行后所有隐藏集任务均被解决，平均成功时间为2.7轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮代码生成指标无法捕捉部署型智能体的核心能力——在收到具体反馈后修复错误工件的能力。现有评估方法缺乏对多轮细粒度修复过程的系统度量框架。

Method: A-CEGIS框架采用"提出-检查-反馈-再生成"的迭代循环：智能体提出正则表达式，确定性oracle在全匹配语义下检查，并生成紧凑的假阳性或假阴性见证（counterexample）作为下一轮的指导性反馈。

Result: 在30个NL-RX-Turk任务上，诊断性反例反馈在四轮消融预算内解决90%任务（零样本17%、通用自我修正27%、仅错误反馈23%）；完整诊断运行中，隐藏集全部任务在最终轮前被解决，平均时间到成功为2.7轮，定向探测后稳健成功率为77%。

Conclusion: A-CEGIS能够度量智能体跨轮次的改进效率，并提供超出原始留出测试集的实用鲁棒性检查手段。

Abstract: Single-turn code-generation metrics understate a central property of deployed agents: whether they can repair a wrong artifact after receiving concrete feedback. This paper presents A-CEGIS, a lightweight framework that uses counterexamples as feedback for evaluating multi-turn refinement in natural-language-to-regex synthesis. An agent proposes a regex, a deterministic oracle checks it under full-match semantics, and compact false-positive or false-negative witnesses guide the next turn. On 30 NL-RX-Turk tasks, diagnostic counterexample feedback solves 90\% of tasks within a four-turn ablation budget, compared with 17% for zero-shot generation, 27% for generic self-correction, and 23% for error-only feedback. In a full diagnostic run with hardening, all tasks are solved on the hidden set by the final turn, with mean time-to-success of 2.7 turns and robust success of 77% after targeted probing. These results show that A-CEGIS measures how efficiently an agent improves across turns while adding a practical robustness check beyond the original held-out cases.

</details>


### [106] [Probe Generalization as Subspace Selection for OOD Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02893)
*Daniel Yoo,Adrians Skapars*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型激活中探针（linear probes）的跨域泛化问题，发现将输入投影到训练分布激活的主成分（PCs）子集上可以实现近于直接在测试域训练探针的跨域迁移性能。进一步利用LLM法官对PC的语义解释进行筛选，选出可迁移的方向，在Insider Trading Report上缩小基线到oracle差距的78%，在Sandbagging上缩小25%。结果表明探针的OOD鲁棒性主要由子空间选择决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针常用于检测语言模型激活中的行为与概念，但面临跨分布（out-of-distribution）泛化失败的挑战。该论文希望理解探针在检测欺骗（deception）行为时为何无法迁移到不同数据集，并探索如何选择可迁移的子空间方向以提升跨域性能。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，跨3个held-out欺骗检测数据集评估探针泛化。首先将输入激活投影到训练分布的主成分（PC）子集上，发现该操作带来近oracle级别的跨域迁移。随后利用LLM法官对每个PC的最/最不激活示例进行语义打分，评估其是否蕴含可迁移的欺骗方向，再在高分PC上训练探针。

Result: 基于PC子集的投影即可使跨域迁移近乎达到直接在测试域训练探针的效果。结合LLM法官的PC语义筛选，在Insider Trading Report上将基线到oracle的差距缩小78%，在Sandbagging上缩小25%。分析发现源探针权重较重加载的方向偏向源域表层特征，而真正可迁移的方向以更抽象的方式编码同一对比，且可由自然语言描述捕捉。

Conclusion: 探针的OOD鲁棒性在很大程度上取决于子空间选择而非探针本身。PC分解结合语义解释提供了一种系统化的子空间筛选策略，使跨域概念检测更为可靠。

Abstract: Linear probes can be used to detect behaviors and concepts inside language model activations, but may fail to transfer to out-of-distribution examples. When studying the generalization performance of Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct probes over 3 held-out deception detection datasets, we find that projecting inputs onto a small subset of principal components (PCs) from the training distribution of activations enables cross-domain transfer that nearly matches the performance of probes trained directly on the test distribution. Furthermore, we find that PC interpretations can be used to find a subset of those transferable PCs. By using an LLM judge to score each PC on whether its most/ least activating examples imply a transferable deception direction, then probing on the highest-scoring PCs, we close the baseline-to-oracle gap by 78% on Insider Trading Report and by 25% on Sandbagging. The directions a source probe weights heavily appear to encode source-specific surface features, while the directions that actually transfer appear to encode the same contrast more abstractly, in a way natural language descriptions can capture. Broadly, our results suggest that the OOD robustness of probes is largely determined by subspace selection.

</details>


### [107] [R$^{2}$Adapter: A Routing and Rewriting Adapter for Efficient Hybrid RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02894)
*Yucan Guo,Miao Su,Saiping Guan,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Zixuan Li,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出R²Adapter，一个轻量级的即插即用路由和重写适配器，用于在vanilla RAG和图谱RAG之间动态分配查询，将图谱RAG的使用率降低高达59%，同时保持相当的答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统面临困境：vanilla RAG对简单查询高效但对多跳推理困难，图谱RAG解决了多跳问题但推理复杂度和延迟更高。实际用户查询复杂度差异大，固定RAG策略并非最优。现有混合文本-图谱RAG方法依赖启发式规则和LLM路由，带来不必要开销并强依赖底层LLM。作者希望实现一个模型无关、轻量、自适应的混合RAG路由方案。

Method: 提出R²Adapter，一个轻量级Plug-in路由与重写适配器：(1) 通过路由机制动态判断哪些查询真正需要图谱推理，只对受益于图谱推理的查询进行图谱检索，减少不必要的图谱检索开销；(2) 对路由到图谱但不确定的查询进行重写，更好地暴露其多跳推理需求，在无需额外监督的情况下提升检索质量。该适配器与底层模型无关，可无缝集成到多种vanilla和基于图谱的RAG流程中。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准上的实验表明，R²Adapter将图谱RAG的使用率降低高达59%，同时保持可比的答案准确率，验证了其在自适应混合RAG系统中的高效性。

Conclusion: R²Adapter提供了一种高效、自适应的混合RAG方案，通过精准路由和查询重写，在保证答案质量的前提下显著降低图谱检索的计算开销，且模型无关、可即插即用地集成到现有RAG流程中。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a prevailing paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with non-parametric knowledge. Vanilla RAG efficiently handles simple queries but struggles with relational or multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based RAG alleviates this issue but incurs higher inference complexity and latency. In practice, user queries can differ significantly in their complexity, rendering a fixed RAG strategy suboptimal. However, existing hybrid text-graph RAG methods typically rely on heuristic and LLM-based routing, resulting in unnecessary overhead and strong dependence on the underlying LLM. To address these challenges, we propose R$^{2}$Adapter, a lightweight plug-in Routing and Rewriting Adapter designed to allocate queries between vanilla and graph-based RAG dynamically. By routing only the queries that genuinely benefit from graph-based reasoning, R$^{2}$Adapter reduces unnecessary graph retrieval overhead. Additionally, uncertain graph-routed queries are rewritten to better expose their multi-hop reasoning requirements, improving retrieval quality without additional supervision. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that R$^{2}$Adapter reduces graph-based RAG usage by up to 59% while maintaining comparable answer accuracy. This adapter is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into diverse vanilla and graph-based RAG pipelines, providing an efficient and adaptive solution for hybrid RAG systems.

</details>


### [108] [BharatGather: A Culturally-Informed Benchmark Dataset for Misinformation and Fake News Detection in Indian Public Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02895)
*Parth Bramhecha,Smit Deshmukh,Sairaj Bodhale,Adwait Borate,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍BharatGather，一个专为印度大规模公共活动中虚假信息二分类检测而构建的多来源数据集，包含14,646条记录，通过网络爬取事实核查平台、多媒体转录提取和LLM合成增强的混合管道构建，旨在为文化敏感的事件感知式虚假信息检测提供基准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模公共活动（如宗教节日、政治集会和文汇聚会）日益面临虚假信息快速传播的威胁，对公共安全和社会凝聚力构成重大风险。尽管自动化虚假信息检测在方法上取得显著进展，但现有基准数据集通常无法捕捉印度语境特有的社会文化细微差别和事件特定动态，导致现有模型在印度大规模集会场景中效果欠佳。

Method: 论文构建了一个混合数据管道：(1) 系统化地从知名事实核查平台进行网页抓取；(2) 提取多媒体转录内容；(3) 利用大语言模型（LLM）进行合成增强以扩充叙事的多样性。最终生成包含14,646条记录的语料库，用于二分类虚假信息检测。

Result: 成功构建了BharatGather数据集，包含14,646条精心策划的多来源记录，覆盖印度大规模公共集会场景，为事件感知式虚假信息检测提供了文化适配的基准资源。

Conclusion: BharatGather通过提供针对印度独特复杂性的资源，促进了文化知悉型检测系统的发展，并为高风险的公共环境中评估这些系统的性能建立了严格的基准框架。

Abstract: Large-scale public events, such as religious festivals, political rallies, and cultural gatherings, are increasingly vulnerable to the rapid dissemination of misinformation, posing substantial risks to public safety and social cohesion. While automated fake news detection has seen significant methodological progress, existing benchmarks frequently fail to capture the socio-cultural nuances and event-specific dynamics characteristic of the Indian context. This paper introduces BharatGather, a curated, multi-source dataset specifically engineered for binary misinformation classification within the ecosystem of Indian mass gatherings. The corpus comprises 14,646 records constructed through a hybrid pipeline involving systematic web scraping of prominent fact-checking platforms, multimedia transcript extraction, and Large Language Model (LLM)-mediated synthetic augmentation to ensure narrative diversity. By providing a resource tailored to the unique complexities of event-aware misinformation in India, this work facilitates the development of culturally informed detection systems and establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating their performance in high-stakes public environments.

</details>


### [109] [PiPMRE: A Pipeline Based on Language Model for Medical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02896)
*Jiaxin Duan,Fengyu Lu,Junfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PiPMRE，一个基于语言模型的医学关系抽取流水线框架，通过关系生成器和关系过滤器两步完成关系抽取，无需设计标注方案，在两个公开数据集上超越SOTA平均5.6个recall点和4.4个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将医学关系抽取视为序列标注任务，但其标注方案设计具有挑战性，且由于医学实体间关系复杂，常导致无法抽取多个关系。因此需要从语言学视角出发探索新的方法。

Method: 提出PiPMRE流水线框架，由关系生成器和关系过滤器组成。生成器先产出多个关系三元组，过滤器对每个三元组打分并仅保留通过阈值的结果。不依赖标注方案，而是使用简单模板重写输入文本，使实体和关系以上下文顺序生成，并基于语言模型构建。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明PiPMRE优于先前方法，平均超越SOTA 5.6个recall点和4.4个准确率点，并且在少样本设置下也展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: PiPMRE通过生成-过滤的流水线范式有效解决了传统序列标注方法在医学关系抽取中的局限，证明了该方法的有效性和泛化能力，即使在数据稀缺的场景下也能保持优势。

Abstract: Medical relation extraction (MRE) is commonly known for extracting entities and their relations jointly from a medical text, which has attracted considerable attention in recent years. Previous studies treat MRE as a sequence tagging task, which results in either a challenging design of the tagging schema or a failed extraction of multiple relations, due to intricate relationships among medical entities. In this work, we review the task from the linguistic perspective and propose a novel pipeline framework, PiPMRE, developed on language models to enhance MRE performance. Specifically, PiPMRE consists of a relation generator and a relation filter. Given a text, the generator first yields multiple relational triplets, and then the filter scores each triplet and retains only those that pass the borderline as the final results. Implementing PiPMRE requires no tagging schema; instead, we use a simple template to reformulate the input text, ensuring that entities and relations are generated in a contextual order. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate the advancement of PiPMRE. It surpasses the previous state-of-the-art by an average of 5.6 recall points and 4.4 accuracy points. PiPMRE's superiority is also demonstrated in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [110] [Margins, Not Windows: Training-Free Per-Step Lossy Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02897)
*Oszkár Urbán,Young D. Kwon,Stylianos I. Venieris,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaptiveSpec是一种免训练的逐步投机解码方法，通过在解码过程中自适应调整两个关键决策（token匹配验证规则和草稿树形状）来加速LLM推理，在SGLang引擎上相比EAGLE-3最高提升56%吞吐量，并在多个基准任务上恢复93%至无损的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法（如EAGLE-3等树形草稿器）通常固定两个决策：严格的token匹配验证规则和静态草稿树形状。虽然已有研究分别放松这些约束，但它们都是在孤立且假设受限条件下进行的（如需要长草稿链的免训练有损验证、固定token预算下的自适应树形结构）。缺乏一种能够同时在推理过程中自适应调整这两个决策的方法。

Method: AdaptiveSpec提出两个正交的自适应机制：(1) 逐步margin规则——当目标模型对草稿token的概率与其top-1概率之比超过阈值时，允许接受不匹配的草稿token，该规则不依赖草稿长度或底层草稿器架构；(2) 逐步树策略——融合草稿top-1置信度与滚动接受历史（反映近期草稿与目标的一致性），动态调整草稿树的深度、宽度和节点数量，使总草稿数量可变而非仅重新分配。两种机制作用于正交维度并产生复合增益。在SGLang生产级推理引擎上实现。

Result: 在SGLang引擎上，AdaptiveSpec相比最先进的投机解码方法EAGLE-3，在三个目标模型（DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B）上的GSM8K、MATH-500和HumanEval任务中实现最高56%的吞吐量提升，同时恢复93%至完全无损的任务准确率。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSpec证明同时自适应草稿验证规则和草稿树形状可以带来显著的投机解码加速，且不需要额外训练。该方法与具体草稿器架构解耦，可应用于生产级推理系统，在保持任务准确率的同时大幅提升吞吐量。两种自适应机制的协同作用（复合效应）优于各自单独作用。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting candidate tokens and verifying them in parallel. Tree-attention drafters such as EAGLE-3 are widely adopted, yet typically hold two decisions fixed: (1) a strict token-match verification rule and (2) a static draft-tree shape. Prior work relaxes each in isolation under limiting assumptions: long draft chains for training-free lossy verification, and adaptive tree shaping under a fixed token budget. We introduce AdaptiveSpec, a training-free per-step speculative decoding method that adapts both decisions from internal signals already produced during decoding. A per-step margin rule promotes a mismatched draft-proposed token when the ratio of the target's probability on the drafted token to its top-1 probability exceeds a threshold with no dependence on draft length or underlying drafter architecture. A per-step tree policy adjusts the draft tree's depth, width, and node count directly from a fused signal of draft top-1 confidence and a rolling acceptance history capturing recent draft-target agreement, allowing the total draft count to vary rather than only be redistributed. The two adaptations operate on orthogonal axes and compound in effect. Implemented on the SGLang production-grade serving engine, AdaptiveSpec improves throughput over the state-of-the-art autoregressive speculative decoding method EAGLE-3 by up to 56%, recovering 93% to fully lossless task accuracy across GSM8K, MATH-500, and HumanEval on three target models (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B).

</details>


### [111] [Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET): Parameter-Efficient Biomedical Domain Adaptation via Priority-Based Embedding Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02898)
*Girish Sundaram,Daniel Berleant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET)，一种知识迁移范式，将BioBERT等大型领域专用模型的生物医学领域知识注入小型通用模型DistilBERT（66M参数），无需在原始专用语料上重新训练。DRET采用迭代策略家族（统一分词器合并、混合嵌入平均、基于优先级的嵌入迁移、嵌入层冻结/差分学习率/标签传播/类别不平衡感知损失），在EBM-NLP语料上的PICO token级分类任务中，以远小于大型模型的参数量达到媲美甚至超越的平衡准确率、召回率和ROC-AUC，并通过余弦相似度、语义漂移和t-SNE分析证实迁移发生在嵌入层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型领域专用语言模型（如BioBERT、ClinicalBERT）在生物医学NLP任务上表现优异，但其计算需求使其难以在实际部署中应用。相比之下，通用型轻量模型（如DistilBERT）虽然高效但缺乏领域知识，难以胜任PICO分类等专业任务。现有方法与效率之间缺乏兼顾路径是核心动机。

Method: DRET以迭代范式家族实现知识迁移：(1) DRET 1.x采用统一分词器合并策略；(2) DRET 2.0使用混合嵌入平均；(3) DRET 3.x基于优先级从最有权威的源模型中分层选择嵌入进行迁移；(4) DRET 4.x进一步结合嵌入层冻结、差分学习率、标签传播和类别不平衡感知损失函数。评估使用EBM-NLP语料下的token级PICO分类，面对严重的类别不平衡，并使用十二项指标体系全面评测。

Result: DRET增强的DistilBERT（66M参数）在平衡准确率、召回率和ROC-AUC上与比其大一个数量级的模型竞争性相当，且在多个类别维度指标上实现超越；同时保持DistilBERT的运行效率。余弦相似度、语义漂移和t-SNE分析证实知识迁移确实发生在嵌入层面。

Conclusion: DRET提供了一种可扩展、资源高效、接近领域专家性能的路径，适用于生物医学文本挖掘，可直接应用于自动化系统文献综述和临床决策支持系统。

Abstract: Large domain-specific language models such as BioBERT and ClinicalBERT achieve strong performance on biomedical NLP tasks, but their computational demands make them impractical for many real-world deployments. General-purpose, parameter-efficient models such as DistilBERT are lightweight yet lack the domain knowledge required for specialized tasks such as PICO (Population, Intervention, Comparison, Outcome) classification. We introduce Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET), a knowledge-transfer paradigm that injects biomedical domain knowledge from large specialized models into a smaller general-purpose model without retraining on the original specialized corpora. DRET is developed as an iterative family of strategies: a unified tokenizer-merge strategy (DRET 1.x), hybrid embedding averaging (DRET 2.0), and a priority-based embedding-transfer mechanism (DRET 3.x) that hierarchically selects embeddings from the most authoritative source models, further combined with embedding-layer freezing, differential learning rates, label propagation, and imbalance-aware loss functions (DRET 4.x). We evaluate DRET on token-level PICO classification using the EBM-NLP corpus under severe class imbalance, across a twelve-metric battery. DRET-enhanced DistilBERT (66M parameters) attains balanced accuracy, recall, and ROC-AUC competitive with, and on several class-wise metrics exceeding, models an order of magnitude larger, while retaining DistilBERT's efficiency. We further show that transfer occurs at the embedding level through cosine-similarity, semantic-shift, and t-SNE analyses. DRET offers a scalable, resource-efficient route to near-domain-expert performance for biomedical text mining, with direct application to automated systematic literature reviews and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [112] [Contamination Inflates Scores but Rarely Reorders Large Language Model Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02899)
*Xingyao Xiao,Yihong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基准污染问题，提出将污染重构为锚点-项目不变性的违反，并通过原题与语义等价改写题的性能差异来隔离记忆与能力。利用47个公开发布模型及74个注入已知污染剂量的微调模型，在四个基准上校准测量工具后，发现公开模型间的污染是均匀的：它抬高绝对分数但不改变排行榜排序（秩相关0.997），仅有极少数模型-基准组合表现出会改变排名的差异化污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准污染（测试项目泄漏到训练数据）一直被视作威胁LLM排行榜可靠性的主要问题，但现有讨论混淆了两个不同的问题：污染是否抬高绝对分数，以及它是否重排模型排名。作者希望通过区分这两个问题，消除对污染的过度焦虑，并提供量化方法。

Method: 将污染定义为锚点-项目不变性的违反，通过比较原版项目与语义等价改写项目（paraphrased items）的成绩差异来测量，这种组内对比使被测量的技能保持固定，从而从能力中分离记忆效应。使用47个公开发布模型和74个注入已知污染剂量的微调模型，横跨ARC、GSM8K、HellaSwag、MMLU四个基准，首先用真实污染标签校准测量工具，再进行排行榜影响分析，包括秩相关检验、敏感性分析及多重参考交叉验证。

Result: 校准结果表明测量工具能剂量响应式恢复注入的污染（测试集泄漏修正效应+0.187），且对仅用合法训练分割训练的阴性对照模型不误报（-0.012）。标准排行榜与改写控制排行榜之间的秩相关为0.997；188个模型-基准组合中仅3个表现出被两个参考交叉验证的差异化污染，远低于改变排名所需的水平。因此公开模型间的污染大体均匀，抬高绝对分数但基本不改变排名排序。

Conclusion: 污染对排行榜排名的影响被严重高估：其对绝对分数的整体抬升不等于对排名的扭曲，排名扰动需要罕见的差异化污染。作者建议排行榜应报告改写控制排名（paraphrase-controlled rankings）并附带置信区间，并发布了可复现的校准不变性审计参考实现。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination, the leakage of test items into training data, is widely described as a threat to the reliability of large language model (LLM) leaderboards. We argue that this concern conflates two distinct questions: whether contamination inflates absolute scores, and whether it reorders the ranking of models. We recast contamination as a violation of anchor-item invariance and measure it through the differential functioning of original versus semantically equivalent paraphrased items, a within-item contrast that holds the measured skill fixed and isolates memorization from capability. Using per-instance responses from 47 publicly released models and 74 models finetuned with a known dose of contamination, across four benchmarks (ARC, GSM8K, HellaSwag, MMLU), we first calibrate the measure against ground truth: it recovers injected contamination dose-responsively (a corrected effect of +0.187 accuracy points for test-set leakage) and never flags a negative-control model trained only on the legitimate training split (-0.012). We then quantify leaderboard impact: the rank correlation between a standard leaderboard and a paraphrase-controlled leaderboard is 0.997, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the observed differential contamination is far below the level needed to move rankings, with only 3 of 188 model-by-benchmark cases showing differential contamination corroborated across two references. Contamination among these public models is therefore largely uniform: it inflates absolute scores without reordering the leaderboard, and ranking distortion requires the rare case of differential contamination. We provide a calibrated invariance audit, released as a reference implementation, and recommend that leaderboards report paraphrase-controlled rankings alongside confidence intervals.

</details>


### [113] [Dual-Form ASR: Semantics-Aware Inverse Text Normalization for Chinese Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02901)
*Fengrun Zhang,Li Fu,Wangjin Zhou,Lu Fan,Youzheng Wu,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-ASR（双格式语音识别）框架，通过配对的口语形式和书面形式监督将ASR与逆文本规范化（ITN）联合建模，并采用LLM驱动的生成-评判流程构建训练数据，引入ITN-MWER序列级目标和REQUIRE-ITN/FORBID-ITN决策感知评估协议，在中文SpeechIO基准上显著优于开源ASR-ITN级联系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR-ITN采用级联模块架构，将语音识别与文本规范化分离，导致：(1) 转录错误会在ITN阶段传播放大；(2) 规范化过程与声学-上下文建模脱节，尤其难以处理语义相关的数字表达。现有方案无法在单个框架内同时保证口语形式转录的忠实性和书面形式的可读性。

Method: 提出DF-ASR框架，核心包括：(1) 通过配对的口语形式和书面形式监督扩展ASR能力，支持提示级选择转录格式；(2) 利用大语言模型（LLM）驱动的生成-评判工作流构造双格式监督数据；(3) 提出ITN-MWER序列级目标函数，对规范化敏感片段的错误赋予更高代价；(4) 引入决策感知的REQUIRE-ITN/FORBID-ITN协议，分别评估必需的规范化操作与禁止改动片段的保持能力。

Result: 在SpeechIO人工标注中文子集上，DF-ASR稳定超越开源ASR-ITN系统，与强闭源参考系统竞争力相当，并能保持可靠的口语/书面格式提示级控制。

Conclusion: 将ITN从级联后处理模块融入ASR的端到端联合建模是可行且有效的方向，双格式监督与规范化敏感的序列级训练目标能够显著提升书面格式输出的鲁棒性，同时不损害口语格式转录的原有能力。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) scenarios require both spoken-form transcripts for faithful transcription and readable written-form transcripts with inverse text normalization (ITN). However, these forms are typically produced by cascaded modules, where a spoken-form ASR output is rewritten by a separate ITN component, making written-form ASR-ITN vulnerable to recognition errors and decoupling normalization from acoustic-contextual modeling, especially for semantically dependent numeric expressions. In this paper, we propose Dual-Form ASR (DF-ASR), a framework that extends spoken-form ASR capability to semantics-aware written-form ITN through paired spoken-form and written-form supervision while retaining prompt-level selection between transcript forms. The dual-form supervision is constructed via a large language model (LLM)-driven generate-and-judge workflow, and training is further enhanced by ITN-MWER, a sequence-level objective that assigns higher cost to errors on normalization-sensitive spans. We also introduce a decision-aware REQUIRE-ITN/\FORBID-ITN protocol to separately measure required normalization and forbidden-span preservation. On manually annotated Chinese subsets from SpeechIO, DF-ASR consistently outperforms open-source ASR-ITN systems, remains competitive with strong closed-source references, and preserves reliable prompt-level control between spoken-form and written-form outputs.

</details>


### [114] [RL-ADA: A World-Feedback Framework for Adversarially Robust Enterprise Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02902)
*Ram Narayanan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RL-ADA是一个共进化对抗训练框架，通过世界反馈（基于可测量交互结果的奖励信号）替代人工标注，消除企业客服对话代理的标注瓶颈。3B参数的客服代理与7B参数的对抗客户代理在固定自动化评判指导下共进化，隔离训练场迭代地重新训练较弱代理。在银行客服概念验证中，工具路由错误被消除，端到端PASS率在五个共进化周期内翻倍，全程无标注数据驱动。该过程还涌现出"情境伪装"对抗策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客服对话代理面临持续的标注瓶颈：鲁棒训练需要大规模标注交互数据，但企业对话日志隐私敏感且标注昂贵，同时用户行为演进速度远超标注流水线。这导致无法高效部署高质量的任务导向对话系统。

Method: 提出RL-ADA共进化训练框架：1) 3B参数客服代理(DA)与7B参数对抗客户代理(CA)在对抗竞技场中共同进化；2) 使用固定自动化评判，DA因正确完成多轮对话至成功解决而获得奖励，CA因产生逼真且隐藏意图（导致误路由）的话语而获得奖励，通过独立结构的对立奖励形成不对称对抗压力；3) 隔离训练场基于先前失败记录迭代性地重新训练较弱代理，全程无需人工标注。

Result: 在银行客服概念验证中，工具路由错误被完全消除，严格端到端PASS率在五个共进化周期内翻倍，完全由自动化竞技场奖励驱动，无需任何标注数据。此外，观察到"情境伪装"（Contextual Camouflage）策略的涌现：CA仅凭奖励压力就学会将意图嵌入密集的真实客户细节中。

Conclusion: RL-ADA展示了以世界反馈替代人工标注的可行性，可消除企业客服代理的标注瓶颈。涌现的对抗策略（如情境伪装）对企业的红队测试和鲁棒性评估具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: Deploying task-oriented dialogue agents in enterprise customer support faces a persistent annotation bottleneck: robust training requires labelled interaction data at scale, yet enterprise conversational logs are privacy-sensitive and expensive to annotate, while user behaviour evolves faster than labelling pipelines can keep pace. We present RL-ADA (Reinforcement Learning with Adversarial Dialogue Agents), a co-evolutionary training framework that eliminates this bottleneck by replacing human labels with \emph{world feedback}: consequence-based reward signals derived directly from measurable interaction outcomes. A Customer Support Agent (DA, 3B parameters) and an Adversarial Customer Agent (CA, 7B parameters) co-evolve in an adversarial arena guided by a fixed automated judge: the DA is rewarded for correctly handling multi-turn customer conversations to successful resolution, while the CA is rewarded for producing realistic, intent-concealing utterances that cause misroutes, creating asymmetric adversarial pressure through opposing but independently structured rewards. An isolation gym iteratively retrains the weaker agent on prior-failure transcripts, requiring no human annotation at any stage. In a banking customer support proof of concept, tool-routing errors are eliminated and the strict end-to-end PASS rate doubles over five co-evolutionary cycles, driven solely by automated arena reward with no labelled data. We additionally observe the emergence of \textbf{Contextual Camouflage}, an adversarial strategy in which the CA learns to embed intent within dense realistic customer detail purely from reward pressure, with direct implications for enterprise red-teaming and robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [115] [Listen to the Latents: Self-Correcting Speech Recognition in Large Audio Language Models Through Hidden-State Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02940)
*Chan-Jan Hsu,Jaeyeon Kim,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shinji Watanabe,Hung-yi Lee,Carlos Busso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid Search策略，通过计算LLM-based ASR模型与基础LLM隐藏状态之间的交互特征来识别语义依赖度较高的token，并针对性地利用基础LLM进行推理时纠错，从而在LoRA适配场景下进一步提升ASR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统通常通过logit融合（外部）或warm initialization（内部）两种方式整合LLM，但如何有效结合这两种策略尚未被充分探索。作者希望挖掘warm-initialized模型中保留的基础LLM的剩余价值。

Method: 提出Hybrid Search方法：(1) 通过LLM-based ASR隐藏状态与基础LLM隐藏状态之间的交互特征作为信号，衡量每个token的语义依赖程度；(2) 仅对语义依赖度高的token进行选择性纠错，而非全局应用LLM的rescoring或late fusion方法。该方法适用于LoRA适配场景（基础LLM权重被保留的情况）。

Result: 实验表明，针对高语义依赖token的选择性纠错性能远超朴素全局LLM纠错方法（包括rescoring和late fusion）。这验证了即使经过warm initialization完成语义知识迁移后，ASR模型仍可通过其基础LLM进一步提升推理时的性能。

Conclusion: 本文分析揭示了一个关键洞见：warm initialization并非语义知识利用的终点，LLM-based ASR模型在推理阶段仍可借助其基础LLM获得性能增益。精确识别并选择性纠错高语义依赖token是一种更有效的推理时增强策略。

Abstract: Recent automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems increasingly integrate large language models (LLMs) to leverage their semantic knowledge, either externally through logit fusion or internally through warm initialization. However, how to effectively combine these two strategies remains underexplored. In this work, we refine warm-initialized LLM-based ASR models by leveraging their own pre-adaptation base LLMs, focusing on LoRA-adapted settings where the base LLM is preserved. To achieve this, we propose Hybrid Search, a targeted correction strategy motivated by two observations. First, interaction features that characterize the relationship between LLM-based ASR hidden states and base-LLM hidden states provide informative signals about a token's degree of semantic dependence. Second, selectively refining targeted tokens with high semantic dependence improves ASR performance far beyond naive global LLM-correction methods including rescoring and late fusion. Our analysis suggests that, even after semantic knowledge transfer through warm initialization, LLM-based ASR models can still leverage their base LLM to further improve inference-time performance.

</details>


### [116] [Judging LLM-as-a-Judge: Concerning Rubric Artifacts in LLM-based Automated Text Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02942)
*Anshul Bagaria,Sowmya S Sundaram,Gokul S Krishnan,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM-as-a-Judge评估管道的一个关键问题：仅基于rubric文本训练的分类器无需访问被评估内容即可部分预测评分，且在对候选回复或rubric标准进行反事实翻转时，法官模型往往无法可靠更新决策，质疑了基于rubric的LLM评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge管道基于一个隐含假设：评分判断源自模型对候选回复相对于rubric标准进行推理。作者认为这一假设值得进一步审视，并系统性地检验其有效性。

Method: 作者采用三种方法：1）训练仅使用rubric文本、不接触任何被评估回复的分类器来预测法官输出；2）通过反事实扰动（分别翻转候选回复方向和rubric标准方向）考察法官模型是否相应地更新决策。

Result: 仅基于rubric文本训练的分类器即可获得非平凡的预测表现，说明rubric表述本身编码了可恢复的评估信号，评分可部分独立于模型输出被预先预期；此外，反事实扰动实验显示法官在面对候选回复或rubric标准反转时经常无法可靠地更新其决策。

Conclusion: rubric中嵌入的先验评估偏差与法官对输入变化的不充分敏感性共同构成对基于rubric的LLM评估可靠性的严重担忧，需要进一步的方法论研究来改进自动化评估。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge pipelines are increasingly used to evaluate AI-generated text, based on the assumption that judgments arise from reasoning over candidate responses with respect to a rubric. We show that this assumption warrants further scrutiny. Classifiers trained only on rubric text, without access to any evaluated response, achieve nontrivial predictive performance on judge outputs. This suggests that rubric formulations encode recoverable evaluative signals, allowing scores to be partially anticipated independently of model outputs. Finally, counterfactual perturbations reveal that judges often fail to reliably update their decisions when either the candidate response or the rubric criterion is reversed. Our findings raise concerns about the reliability of rubric-based LLM evaluation and highlight the need for further methodological study of automated evaluation via LLMs.

</details>


### [117] [LexIssue: Benchmarking Legal Issue Identification in Chinese Civil Litigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02954)
*Huiyuan Xie,Yuqin Huang,Zhicheng Hao,Yida Cai,Shaochun Wang,Zhenghao Liu,Yuxiao Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究诉讼中法律争议焦点的计算建模，提出法律层级模式将争议焦点表示为自由文本描述和结构化法律类别两个层面，构建了包含430个真实中国民事诉讼案例和1303个专家标注争议焦点的LexIssue基准，并开发了覆盖27个案由441个法律争议条目的知识库以支持检索增强推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别诉讼双方争议的法律焦点是实际诉讼中的关键环节，但在法律AI研究中相对未被充分探索。现有研究对法律争议焦点的计算建模关注不足。

Method: 提出法律争议焦点识别为两个互补任务：争议焦点生成和争议焦点分类。构建LexIssue基准数据集、开发问题中心的法律知识库，采用检索增强生成（RAG）方法进行推理。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验表明，使用所构建法律争议知识库的检索增强生成方法在识别争议焦点及其法律属性方面持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 法律争议焦点知识库驱动的检索增强生成方法能有效提升诉讼争议焦点的识别能力，验证了将领域知识与生成模型结合的价值。

Abstract: Identifying the issues disputed between litigating parties is a crucial component of real-world litigation. However, legal issues remain comparatively underexplored in legal AI research. In this work, we study the computational modelling of legal issue identification in litigation. We introduce a legally grounded hierarchical schema that represents legal issues through both free-form issue descriptions and structured legal categories, and formulate legal issue identification as two complementary tasks: legal issue generation and legal issue classification. Based on this formulation, we construct LexIssue, a benchmark containing 430 real-world Chinese civil litigation cases and 1,303 expert-annotated disputed legal issues. We further develop an issue-centric legal knowledge base spanning 27 causes of action and 441 candidate legal issue entries to support retrieval-augmented reasoning. Experimental results across a diverse set of models show that retrieval-augmented generation using the constructed legal issue knowledge base consistently improves performance in identifying disputed legal issues and their corresponding legal attributes.

</details>


### [118] [Unifying Conformal Language Tasks with In-Context Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03005)
*Xiao Shi Huang,Chen-Yuan Lin,Bruce Kuwahara,Kin Kwan Leung,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Relevance框架，通过上下文学习示例筛选和集成方法构建评分函数，在保证覆盖率的条件下提升简洁性，并在7个NLP任务上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多NLP任务（如摘要、抽取式问答）需要在覆盖率和简洁性两个约束下检索相关内容。虽然共形预测方法可以保证覆盖率，但现有最先进的评分函数依赖手工设计LLM提示词，耗费大量人力且针对特定任务。因此需要一种能减少人工干预同时维持覆盖率并提升简洁性的方法。

Method: 提出Conformal Relevance框架，包括两个核心组成部分：(1) 上下文学习中的示例筛选（in-context learning example curation）用于构建评分函数；(2) 集成（ensembling）方法提升评分表现。同时从理论上研究集成对共形分数的影响，提出互补性条件（complementarity condition）刻画集成何时能改善最坏情况下的句子分数，以及集成改进的饱和上界。评分函数设计旨在用最少人工输入同时维持覆盖率并提升简洁性。

Result: 在7个NLP任务上验证了框架的有效性，证明在维持覆盖率的同时改善了简洁性。理论上给出了互补性条件用于刻画集成改善最坏情况句分数的充要条件，以及集成改进的饱和界。

Conclusion: Conformal Relevance通过示例筛选和集成替代手工提示词工程，以最少的人工干预实现覆盖率与简洁性的平衡，并有理论支撑集成策略的影响与边界。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks, such as summarization and extractive question answering, reduce to retrieving relevant content from documents under two constraints: coverage, retaining enough pertinent information to achieve some goal, and conciseness, removing as much irrelevant information as possible. Conformal prediction methods have been used to guarantee coverage, and must be optimized for conciseness through design of a score function. State-of-the-art scoring functions use hand-engineered LLM prompts asking the model to rate the importance of content, but manual prompt engineering is labor-intensive and task-specific. We introduce the Conformal Relevance framework which uses in-context learning example curation and ensembling to create a score function which maintains coverage while improving conciseness with minimal manual input. We demonstrate this framework's application on seven NLP tasks, and also theoretically study the impact of diversity for ensembled conformal scores, giving a complementarity condition that characterizes when ensembling improves worst-case sentence scores, and a saturation bound on ensemble improvement.

</details>


### [119] [SHELF: A Synthetic Harness for Multi-Task Bibliographic Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03047)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHELF是一个用于评估LLM在图书馆和档案馆文献编目工作中表现的开源Python基准测试系统，通过将标注词表、写作规范和生成预算转化为受控基准数据与评估任务。首次发布包含基于美国国会图书馆词表的62,899篇模型生成文档，涵盖分类、聚类、检索等多类任务。结果显示主题分类达0.8887，体裁形式分类仅0.2605，稀疏方法在分类上仍具竞争力，TF-IDF在主题计时实验中速度最快，模型排名可迁移但绝对分数不能直接估计生产数据上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆和档案馆管理大量馆藏但人手与算力资源有限，现有多数基准不系统地测试文献编目工作。机构需要知道哪些方法适用于其特定任务、运行这些方法需要什么样的资源，但缺乏专门的受控评估体系，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: SHELF是一个Python系统，它将带标签的taxonomy（分类词表）、写作规范（writing specifications）和生成预算（generation budget）转化为受控的基准数据与评估任务。首版生成62,899篇模型撰写的文档，基于美国国会图书馆词汇（LCSH等），包含分类、聚类、检索、配对分类和指令检索五类任务。评估方法包括TF、TF-IDF、BM25、主流编码器，以及仅在主题分类上测试的零样本解码器。同时设计独立变化的文献特征维度，并能在模型训练截止后生成新的、可验证的未见文档。

Result: 主题分类达到0.8887的分数，但体裁形式分类仅0.2605，多个配对与聚类任务接近随机水平。稀疏方法（TF、TF-IDF、BM25）在分类任务上仍具竞争力，TF-IDF在主题计时实验中是测量速度最快的方案。SHELF分数与LCSHBench和Project Gutenberg的比较表明，模型排名跨benchmark迁移比绝对分数更可靠，但SHELF分数不能直接估计生产目录数据的准确率。所有源代码和数据均在GitHub和Hugging Face以宽松许可开源发布。

Conclusion: SHELF为图书馆和档案馆提供了首个系统化的LLM评估基准框架，能够根据具体任务和预算约束衡量不同方法的表现。虽然其在分类任务上能有效区分模型优劣且排名具有跨基准的可迁移性，但绝对分数不能直接映射到生产环境准确度，且部分任务（体裁分类、配对与聚类）仍存在较大提升空间，说明该领域评估仍需持续迭代与验证。

Abstract: Libraries and archives manage large collections with limited staff and computing budgets, yet common benchmarks do not systematically test their bibliographic work. They need to know which methods work for their tasks and what those methods require to run. SHELF, the Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness, addresses this gap. It is a Python system that turns labelled taxonomies, writing specifications, and a generation budget into controlled benchmark data and evaluation tasks. This first release contains 62,899 model-written documents based on Library of Congress vocabularies, with tasks for classification, clustering, retrieval, pair classification, and instruction retrieval. We compare TF, TF-IDF, BM25, popular encoders, and, on subject classification only, zero-shot decoders; each method appears only on tasks that support it. Subject classification reaches 0.8887, while genre-form classification reaches only 0.2605, and several pair and clustering tasks remain near chance. Sparse methods remain competitive on classification, while TF-IDF is the fastest measured arm in the subject timing experiment. SHELF also varies bibliographic facets independently and can generate new, verifiably unseen documents after a model's training cutoff. Comparisons with LCSHBench and Project Gutenberg show that model rankings transfer more reliably than absolute scores, but SHELF scores do not estimate accuracy on production catalogue data. We release all source code and data under permissive licenses on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [120] [Large Language Models in Resolving Contextual Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03148)
*Xinye Yang,Zhenyang Liu,Ruisi Li,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究大语言模型处理上下文内部知识冲突（而非参数知识与外部上下文的冲突）的问题，提出六类上下文冲突分类体系并构建包含5,781个样本的ContextConflict数据集（覆盖推理与摘要任务、显式矛盾与隐式冲突）。实验表明九种LLM在解决上下文知识冲突上仍表现不足；机制可解释性分析揭示了模型的隐式冲突意识与表征几何，并发现模型对早出现证据的一致位置偏好是冲突解决的主要障碍。据此提出无需训练和标签的激活引导方法，在推理任务上稳定提升准确率，在摘要任务上生成更高质量且更均衡的摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于LLM内部参数知识与外部给定语境之间的冲突，但忽略了语境自身内部可能产生的知识冲突。作者认为这类上下文内冲突在现实应用中同样普遍且重要（例如同一文档中不同段落或来源的信息相互矛盾），因此需要对此进行系统性定义、评测与调试。

Method: 1) 提出六类上下文冲突分类体系：事实性、推断性、时间性、粒度性、视角性与模糊性冲突；2) 构建ContextConflict数据集（5,781个样本），覆盖推理与摘要两类任务，包含显式矛盾与需多步推理的隐式冲突；3) 在九种LLM上评测冲突解决能力；4) 进行机制可解释性分析，考察模型对冲突的潜在感知与表征几何，并揭示位置偏好偏差；5) 提出一种无需训练、无需标签的激活引导（steering）方法，鼓励模型更全面地整合多方证据以改进冲突解决。

Result: 实验显示当前九种主流LLM在解决上下文知识冲突方面仍表现不足。可解释性分析揭示模型对冲突存在潜在意识，并刻画了冲突处理的表征几何结构；同时发现模型一致偏向较早出现的证据，该位置偏好是冲突有效解决的关键障碍。所提激活引导方法在推理任务上稳定提升准确率，在摘要任务上能生成更高质量、更均衡的摘要。

Conclusion: 上下文内部冲突是当前LLM尚未有效解决的一个重要问题；模型对证据位置（偏早）的系统性偏差是主要瓶颈；通过激活方向引导这一轻量手段即可在不重新训练的情况下显著改善冲突解决能力，为后续研究提供了新方向与基线资源。

Abstract: Most prior works focused on conflicts between an LLM's internal parametric knowledge and externally provided context. In contrast, we investigate how LLMs handle conflicts that arise within contextual knowledge itself. We introduce a taxonomy of six types of contextual conflicts (factual, inferential, temporal, granularity, perspective, and ambiguity) and contribute a comprehensive dataset ContextConflict for this setting. The dataset contains 5,781 samples, covers both reasoning and summarization tasks, and includes both explicit contradictions and implicit conflicts that require multi-step reasoning. Experiments on nine LLMs show that current models still fall short in resolving contextual knowledge conflicts. We further provide mechanistic interpretability insights into how LLMs process such conflicts, revealing their latent awareness of conflicts and the representational geometry underlying conflict processing. In addition, our analysis uncovers a consistent model bias towards earlier evidence, and this positional preference serves as a key obstacle to effective conflict resolution. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a simple training-free, label-free steering method that steers activations to encourage a more comprehensive incorporation of evidences for better conflict resolution. On our dataset, the method consistently improves accuracy on reasoning tasks and generates higher-quality, more balanced summaries for summarization tasks.

</details>


### [121] [No country for old linguists: LLM-brain alignment underdetermines neural computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03160)
*Elliot Murphy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Nastase等人(2026)提出的"LLM可阐明语言加工机制"论点进行批判性分析，核心主张是：表征对齐原则上可约束机制假说，但不能单独识别机制。作者指出Nastase等人从"表征对齐"滑向"共享计算原则"再到"LLM作为完全机制模型"的推理链条存在逻辑、因果和计算上的欠定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nastase等人认为LLM因其分布式、上下文敏感、基于统计学习的表征特性，可阐明大脑语言加工机制。作者认可其对简单皮层"盒状图"式定位的批判，也承认LLM-大脑对齐研究的价值，但质疑其核心推理链条——即从表征对齐能否被合法地扩展为机制层面的主张。

Method: 本文是一篇观点性/批判性论文，采用概念分析和逻辑论证方法。作者通过剖析Nastase等人文中的方法论警示与其最终结论之间的张力，系统论证了推理中的三类欠定问题：(1)逻辑欠定：对齐不足以推出共享机制；(2)因果欠定：相关性不蕴含因果共享；(3)计算欠定：功能等价不蕴含机制等同。

Result: 作者论证了表征对齐（representational alignment）虽然是LLM-大脑研究的有用工具，但它在性质上仅能提供机制假说的约束条件，而非机制本身。Nastase等人一方面承认编码模型不能确立共享架构或算法，另一方面又得出LLM可能体现与生物大脑相同的计算原则并提供"完全机制模型"，这两者之间存在根本张力。

Conclusion: 作者主张对齐证据必须结合其他层面的约束（如神经动力学、因果干预、损伤研究等）才能逼近机制性理解；将对齐直接解读为机制等价，犯了过度推断的错误。LLM可以是重要的启发式工具和假说生成器，但不等同于脑语言的机制模型。

Abstract: Nastase et al. (2026) argue that large language models (LLMs) may illuminate language processing because both rely on distributed, context-sensitive representations shaped by statistical learning. Their rejection of simple cortical "boxology" is persuasive, and they articulate a strong case for the value of LLM-brain alignment research. The key question is what kind of inference LLM-brain alignment licenses. My claim here will be narrow: representational alignment can in principle constrain mechanistic hypotheses, but it does not by itself identify a mechanism. Nastase et al. acknowledge that an encoding model can capture features represented in neural activity without establishing a shared architecture or algorithm. Yet the authors sometime move from alignment to "shared computational principles" and ultimately to LLMs as mechanistic models of natural language. Indeed, their methodological caveat that alignment does not establish a shared architecture or algorithm sits uneasily with their conclusion that LLMs might instantiate the same computational principles as biological brains and provide a "fully mechanistic model" of language. I discuss what I consider to be problems of logical, causal, and computational underdetermination in Nastase et al.'s (2026) proposal.

</details>


### [122] [Jina-OCR-v1: Efficient Document Parsing with Speculative Decoding and Dense Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03181)
*Alejandro Barón García,Feng Wang,Emilia Garcia Casademont,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jina-OCR-v1是一个端到端文档解析模型，面向低预算GPU设计。它融合了DeepSeek-OCR的压缩视觉编码器和3B MoE解码器（每token激活约570M参数），并采用FastMTP投机解码头（K=3，共享单草稿块递归预测），贪心验证保证无损解码。训练结合指令对齐、困难文档鲁棒性微调以及基于密集可验证奖励的GRPO。在OmniDocBench v1.6上达91.14，在olmOCR-Bench上达83.4，页面吞吐量最高达2.57页/秒。在NVIDIA L4上，FastMTP相较贪心自回归解码速度翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析模型通常对硬件资源要求较高，难以在低预算GPU上高效运行。作者旨在构建一个在低预算GPU上既能保持高解析精度又能实现高吞吐量的端到端文档解析模型。

Method: 模型架构上，采用压缩视觉编码器 + 3B MoE解码器（每token激活约570M参数），并集成FastMTP投机解码头：单一草稿块递归共享用于K=3步预测，贪心验证确保无损解码。训练策略上，结合指令对齐、针对困难文档的鲁棒性微调，以及GRPO强化学习（基于公式、表格和结构等确定性验证奖励进行部分计分）。训练数据混合清洗过的公开语料与针对性合成页面。

Result: 在默认动态分辨率设置下，Jina-OCR-v1在OmniDocBench v1.6上获得91.14分，在olmOCR-Bench上获得83.4分，并在对比模型中达到最高页面吞吐量2.57页/秒。在NVIDIA L4低预算GPU上，FastMTP使解码速度较贪心自回归解码提升2倍。

Conclusion: Jina-OCR-v1在低预算GPU条件下实现了高性能文档解析，兼顾了精度、吞吐量和推理效率，验证了MoE稀疏解码、投机采样和奖励驱动后训练在OCR任务中的有效性，模型已公开开源。

Abstract: We present Jina-OCR-v1, an end-to-end document parsing model built to serve on low-budget GPUs. It combines the compressed-vision encoder and the 3B mixture-of-experts decoder of DeepSeek-OCR, which activates about 570M parameters per token, with a FastMTP speculative decoding head that shares a single draft block recursively across K=3 prediction steps. Greedy verification makes decoding lossless. Post-training combines instruction alignment, robustness fine-tuning on difficult documents, and GRPO under dense verifiable rewards: deterministic formula, table, and structural checks that award partial credit. The training data mixes cleaned public corpora with targeted synthetic pages. At the default dynamic-resolution setting, Jina-OCR-v1 scores 91.14 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and 83.4 on olmOCR-Bench, and reaches the highest page throughput in our comparison at 2.57 pages per second. On a low-budget GPU such as the NVIDIA L4, FastMTP doubles decoding speed over greedy autoregressive decoding. The model is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/jinaai/jina-ocr-v1.

</details>


### [123] [MemoryLACE: Memory Lifecycle-Aware Consolidation and Evidence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03201)
*Meriem Yacoubi,Pia Schmidt,Nenad Petrovic,Ahmed Frikha,Martin Kirchhoff,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryLACE (MemLACE)，一种轻量级记忆框架，通过显式建模文本证据的生命周期（稀疏合并、取代、矛盾三种关系）来提升长期LLM代理的记忆推理能力，同时保留原子自然语言记忆及其来源。在BEAM和StructMemEval基准上，使用开源和专有LLM骨干，MemLACE在相同骨干对比中取得最高整体性能，并在BEAM上将端到端运行时间相对最强的反思式基线Hindsight降低66.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期LLM代理面临双重困境：基于文本的记忆系统虽然能高效检索语义相关记忆，但往往将记忆间的关系（如重复证据、历史状态、更新与未解决矛盾）隐式化；而更丰富的结构化方法通过全局图、层次抽象或反思建模这些关系，但复杂度较高。作者希望以轻量方式显式建模文本证据的局部生命周期关系，在不依赖全局知识图谱或全局反思的前提下提升长期记忆推理性能。

Method: MemLACE设计了三类核心关系来建模证据生命周期：稀疏合并（sparse merge）处理重复证据、取代（supersession）处理历史状态更新、矛盾（contradiction）处理未解决冲突。框架保持原子自然语言记忆及其出处（provenance），不将记忆孤立检索，而是重构"关系感知证据单元"，暴露当前证据、历史证据、支持证据和冲突证据以供下游推理使用。方法本质上是局部生命周期建模，替代全局图或全局反思的复杂方案。

Result: 在BEAM和StructMemEval两个基准上，使用开源权重和专有LLM骨干，MemLACE在相同骨干对比中实现了最高整体性能。在BEAM上，相对最强反思式基线Hindsight，端到端运行时间降低66.6%。消融研究表明，生命周期扩展（lifecycle expansion）和时间感知（temporal awareness）是性能提升的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本证据的局部生命周期足以大幅提升长期记忆推理能力，无需构建全面的知识图谱或进行全局反思。这为长期LLM代理的记忆系统设计提供了一条轻量而有效的路径，在性能和效率之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents must preserve information across interactions while distinguishing repeated evidence, historical states, updates, and unresolved contradictions. Existing textual memory systems retrieve semantically relevant memories efficiently but often leave these relationships implicit, whereas richer structured approaches model them through global graphs, hierarchical abstractions, or reflection at greater complexity. We introduce MemoryLACE (MemLACE), a lightweight memory framework that explicitly models the lifecycle of textual evidence through sparse merge, supersession, and contradiction relations while preserving atomic natural-language memories and their provenance. Rather than retrieving memories independently, MemLACE reconstructs relation-aware evidence units that expose current, historical, supporting, and conflicting evidence for downstream reasoning. Across BEAM and StructMemEval, using open-weight and proprietary LLM backbones, MemLACE achieves the highest overall performance in same-backbone comparisons while reducing end-to-end runtime on BEAM by 66.6% relative to Hindsight, the strongest reported reflective-memory baseline. Ablation studies identify lifecycle expansion and temporal awareness as the principal contributors to these gains. Together, the results demonstrate that explicitly modeling the local lifecycle of textual evidence is sufficient to substantially improve long-term memory reasoning without requiring comprehensive knowledge graphs or global reflection.

</details>


### [124] [LLMs Learn Better In-Context from Rules than from Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03213)
*Xiang Fu,Seungmin Cho,Yukyung Lee,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了大型语言模型中两种上下文学习方式——基于规则描述的学习（指令遵循）与基于输入-输出演示的学习（少样本提示）——的学习效能。通过涵盖游戏、算术、语言推理等五个不同领域的学习任务，研究发现模型通常从规则中学习比仅从示例中学习更可靠，额外示例或单纯增加示例数量并不能带来持续且显著的提升。指令微调放大了基于规则学习的优势，而保持基于示例学习能力不变。令人意外的是，基础模型中没有发现基于示例学习的特权效应，甚至规则在代数任务领域仍带来增益。总体而言，当任务需要代数抽象与计算时，规则优于示例的效应更大；而当任务需要分布敏感性和/或调用参数化知识时，该效应较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM中两种主要上下文学习模式（基于规则的指令遵循与基于示例的少样本提示）的相对有效性，并探究影响其学习效能的模型属性和任务属性，以指导如何更有效地利用LLM的上下文学习能力。

Method: 选取五个覆盖不同领域（游戏、算术、语言推理）的学习任务，在相同底层任务的条件下，对比两种学习模式（规则vs示例）的效能；同时系统操纵模型规模和任务属性（如是否需要代数抽象、分布敏感性或参数化知识）来探索调节因素。

Result: （1）模型从规则学习比从纯示例学习更可靠；（2）在规则之上添加示例或简单扩大示例数量并未带来持续显著的收益；（3）指令微调扩大了规则学习的优势，同时保持示例学习能力不受影响；（4）基础模型中没有示例学习的特权效应，规则在代数任务中仍有效；（5）规则相对于示例的优势取决于任务特性：代数抽象任务中更大，需分布敏感性和参数化知识的任务中更小。

Conclusion: LLM的上下文学习中对规则描述的学习总体上优于纯示例学习，指令微调将规则学习的优势进一步放大。规则与示例的相对效能高度依赖任务的计算与知识需求，这为理解上下文学习的机制以及如何设计有效的提示策略提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit in-context learning capabilities, where they can learn new tasks from prompt contexts without weight updates. We compare the learning efficacies of two prominent modes of in-context learning: (1) learning from descriptions of rules (instruction following); and (2) learning from examples of input-output demonstrations (few-shot prompting). Through five learning tasks that cover diverse domains (games, arithmetic, linguistic inferences), we compare two modes of learning (rules vs. examples) specifying the same underlying task. We furthermore explore model and task properties that modulate the learning efficacies. We find that models generally learn more reliably from rules than from examples alone, and additional examples on top of rules or simply scaling up the number of examples do not lead to consistent and significant gains. Instruction tuning amplifies the benefit of rule-based learning while keeping example-based learning capacities intact. Surprisingly, we find no privileged effect of example-based learning in base models, and rules still lead to gains in algebraic task domains. Overall, the comparative efficacy of rules over examples is larger when the task recruits algebraic abstractions and computations, and smaller when the task requires distributional sensitivity and/or recruits parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [125] [SWIM: Student Writing Simulation via Proficiency-Conditioned Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03215)
*Heejin Do,Jakub Kontak,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIM任务，将学生写作模拟形式化为按能力水平条件下的篇章生成，比较提示、SFT和RL三种方法，发现显式监督（SFT、RL）比单纯提示能实现更强的能力画像对齐，但真实低水平写作仍难以复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的学生模拟日益受到关注，但LLM是否能在扩展写作中再现多维度的写作能力差异（内容、组织、词汇、语言）仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 引入SWIM任务，将学生写作模拟构建为基于能力水平条件的文章生成；分别评估提示（含基于评分量规的提示策略）、有监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）方法，并以自动化作文评分衡量画像对齐度。

Result: 提示对能力控制效果有限，即使对强专有LLM亦然——模型能调整内容相关特质，但在风格层面（词汇、语法、组织结构）难以匹配不同能力水平；SFT显著提升对齐度，而使用所提出的能力对齐奖励的RL在各写作特质和提示维度上进一步提升；真实的低能力写作仍然难以复现。

Conclusion: 显式监督相比纯提示可实现更强大的画像对齐；生成真实、可信的低水平学生写作仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Writing proficiency manifests in how students develop content, organize ideas, choose words, and use language. Despite growing interest in LLM-based student simulation, whether LLMs can reproduce such multidimensional variation in extended writing remains largely unexplored. In this work, we explore if language models can realistically simulate student writing, and introduce SWIM, a task that formulates Student Writing sIMulation as proficiency-conditioned essay generation. We evaluate prompting, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) methods for writing simulation using automated essay scoring as a measure of profile alignment. Extensive experiments reveal that prompting provides limited proficiency control, even for strong proprietary LLMs with rubric-grounded strategies. In particular, while models can adjust content-oriented traits, they struggle to reproduce the lexical, grammatical, and organizational variation in different proficiency levels. SFT substantially improves alignment, while RL with the proposed proficiency-alignment reward yields further gains across all writing traits and essay prompts. Our findings suggest that explicit supervision enables substantially stronger profile alignment than prompting alone, while authentic low-proficiency writing remains challenging to reproduce.

</details>


### [126] [The Analyst in the Prompt: Role, Retrieval, and Memory Biases in LLM Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03218)
*Ahmed Asaad,Amr Mohamed,Yang Zhang,Omneya Abdelsalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM在金融领域中使用用户上下文（记忆、画像、角色提示）进行个性化响应时，是否会导致证据驱动的判断产生偏差（即"上下文溢出"）。通过对12个LLM和3,575份SEC文件进行实验，发现大多数偏差源于模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解释差异，而非证据检索差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地将用户上下文（如记忆、画像和角色提示）纳入响应生成过程，这种个性化可能影响基于证据的判断——同一证据在不同用户情境下可能得出不同结论。金融领域因决策常依赖于对长而复杂文档的解释，为研究该问题提供了高风险的场景。

Method: 使用3,575份SEC文件，对12个LLM进行测试。通过三种条件区分证据选择与解释的影响：(1) 角色条件的检索、(2) 中性检索、(3) 记忆框架上下文。提出两种缓解策略：将相同的投资者心态以用户画像而非助手角色的形式表达；并将基于证据的输出与个性化输出分离。

Result: 发现大多数用户上下文溢出效应来源于模型在不同角色下对相同证据的解释差异，而非检索到不同证据。两种缓解策略均能减少溢出效应，但都无法完全消除，且其有效性在不同模型间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 用户上下文对LLM判断的影响主要体现在解释层面而非证据选择层面，简单缓解策略虽能部分抑制该效应，但仍需进一步研究以实现可靠的、个性化与证据严谨性兼顾的LLM系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly use user context such as memory, profiles, and role prompts to personalize their responses. This personalization can affect evidence-based judgment: the same evidence may lead to different conclusions under different user contexts. Finance provides a high-stakes setting to study this problem because decisions often depend on interpreting long and complex documents. We test this using 3,575 SEC filings across twelve LLMs. We compare persona-conditioned retrieval, neutral retrieval, and memory-framed context to separate the effect of evidence selection from the effect of interpretation. We find that most user-context spillover comes from how models interpret the same evidence under different roles, rather than from retrieving different evidence. We then test two simple mitigation strategies: expressing the same investor mindset as a user profile instead of an assistant role, and separating evidence-based and personalized outputs. Both reduce spillover, but neither removes it completely, and their effectiveness varies substantially across models.

</details>


### [127] [Counterfactual Fairness Audits of Multi-Step Clinical LLM Agents Require a Measured Per-Action Instability Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03221)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu,Manpreet Singh,Deepak Parashar,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文表明，反事实审计中常用的"翻转率"（flip rate）本身是不可解释的。研究者通过16个临床场景、每场景10次重复实验发现，即使输入完全相同，临床智能体在8.7%的结果-场景单元中仍会产生动作变化。该不稳定性在不同动作间差异达8倍（ICU升级0.022 vs 受控物质警示0.179），数据中没有任何人口统计对比能与这一基线噪声区分开来。第二个模型产生6.7%的合并基线和几乎相同的动作排序（Spearman 0.94），证明这不是单个系统的现象。多数投票聚合五次抽取可消除39%的噪声，随后趋于平缓；零模拟表明残余噪声源于异质性的单元级概率，因此复制能缓解但不能消除问题。任何未附动作级基线报告的反事实公平性估计都无法作为差异证据。研究用FairMedAgent测量，该框架估计"范围内反事实翻转率"，只计入已发布决策规则允许且经临床医生裁决的动作翻转，需带区裁决，目前未宣称任何差异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实审计是检验临床智能体是否对不同人口统计特征患者差异化处理的标准化工具，报告翻转率以衡量动作在仅改变患者描述符时的变化频率。作者指出该指标单独报告时不具可解释性，需要通过系统性实验揭示其存在不可忽略的固有噪声基线，从而警示研究者不能将翻转率直接解读为偏差证据。

Method: 研究采用FairMedAgent评估框架，在16个临床场景中对同一叙述采用完全相同的描述符进行10次重复运行，测量临床智能体动作的变化率。实验比较了两种不同模型以排除单一系统伪影，使用Spearman秩相关检验动作排序一致性，采用多数投票聚合五轮抽样来测试噪声消除效果，并通过零模拟将残余噪声归因于各单元异质性的翻转概率，同时纳入人口统计属性（种族、性别、年龄、保险、英语熟练度）及其交错组合的固定形式条件。

Result: 相同条件下动作翻转率为8.7%（跨动作异质相差8倍，ICU升级0.022至受控物质警示0.179）；人口统计对比无法与基线区分。第二模型给出6.7%的合并基线和几乎相同的动作排序（Spearman 0.94，p=0.017）。多数投票五轮聚合消除约39%噪声后趋于平稳，零模拟将残余归因于异质性单元概率。

Conclusion: 反事实翻转率若无逐动作基线对照，不能作为偏差证据解读。复制（多数投票）可部分缓解但无法消除不稳定性。研究目前不声称临床智能体存在任何差异结果，需待带区裁决完成后才能得出有效结论。

Abstract: Counterfactual audits are the standard tool for checking whether a clinical agent treats demographically distinct but clinically identical patients differently. They report a flip rate: how often an action changes when only the patient descriptor changes. We show that this quantity is uninterpretable on its own. Re-running an identical condition ten times over sixteen vignettes (same narrative, same descriptor string, nothing varied) moved a clinical agent's action in 8.7% of outcome-vignette cells, and instability was heterogeneous across actions by a factor of eight, from 0.022 for ICU escalation to 0.179 for controlled-substance caution. No demographic contrast in our data was distinguishable from that floor. A second model gives a pooled floor of 6.7% and ranks the six actions almost identically (Spearman 0.94, exact p=0.017), so the floor is not one system's artefact. Majority-vote aggregation over five draws removes 39% of it and then flattens, and a null simulation attributes the residue to heterogeneous per-cell rates, so replication mitigates without eliminating. Any counterfactual fairness estimate reported without a per-action floor beside it therefore cannot be read as evidence of disparity. The measurements were taken with FairMedAgent, an evaluation harness for disparity in the actions of clinical LLM agents whose estimand, the within-range counterfactual flip rate, counts only flips between actions a published decision rule admits and a clinician has adjudicated. That estimand requires band adjudication, which is under way; no disparity result is claimed here. Each synthetic vignette runs a six-stage trajectory (five model-facing decisions around a deterministic environment step) under fixed-form conditions spanning race, sex, age, insurance, English proficiency, and their intersections. The harness, the floor protocol, and every analysis script are released.

</details>


### [128] [SGD-KV: Summarization Guided KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03235)
*Zeyu Liu,Woomin Song,Xuandi Fu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vivek Govindan,Aram Galstyan,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Srikanth Ronanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SGD-KV是一种基于摘要引导的头部感知KV缓存压缩框架，通过分块摘要诊断任务识别擅长层级信息聚合的注意力头并优先分配缓存预算，在长上下文推理中最高减少75%的KV缓存内存，同时保持最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，KV缓存大小随序列长度线性增长，导致严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩技术通常依赖简单启发式方法，忽略了不同注意力头在不同功能角色上的差异，导致压缩效率不高。

Method: 提出SGD-KV框架，由两部分组成：(1) 设计一种新颖的分块摘要诊断任务，通过该任务系统性地识别和优先选择擅长层级信息聚合的注意力头；(2) 基于各注意力头的摘要分数分布策略性地分配KV缓存预算，使预算倾向于对信息聚合更关键的头部。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3-32B上，覆盖多种长上下文基准测试，上下文长度达百万token，KV缓存内存使用减少高达75%，取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 基于注意力头摘要分数分布的KV缓存预算分配方案，能够在长上下文推理中实现更优的效率-精度权衡，证明了头部感知策略在KV缓存压缩中的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face severe memory bottlenecks in long-context inference due to the linearly growing size of key-value (KV) caches. Existing KV cache compression techniques typically rely on simple heuristics, overlooking the distinct functional roles of different attention heads. We present SGD-KV (Summarization-Guided KV Cache Compression), a head-aware framework that leverages a novel chunk-summarization diagnostic task to systematically identify and prioritize attention heads specialized in hierarchical information aggregation. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3-32B across diverse long-context benchmarks demonstrate that SGD-KV achieves state-of-the-art performance with contexts up to 1M tokens, while reducing KV cache memory usage by up to 75%. Our findings show that strategically allocating the KV cache budget based on the summarization score distribution of attention heads yields a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off for long-context inference.

</details>


### [129] [What Else Needs Fixing? Exploring Cost-Effective Test-Time Compute for Revision Propagation in Artifacts Generated Through Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03254)
*Daisuke Kikuta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在对话式生成场景中传播局部修订到工件所有相关部分的能力，提出了一个新的基准测试，评估了九种修订方法（包括顺序反思和并行采样变体）在多个模型上的表现，发现从三路并行样本中选择（基于LLM或质心选择）是最具成本效益的方法，可将准确率提升2.2-9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在对话中往往只指定局部修改，但LLM需要识别工件中的相关依赖关系并将修订传播到所有受影响的部分。这一能力在对话式工件生成中尤为重要，因为工件上下文和依赖关系可能嵌入在对话历史中。然而该能力此前缺乏系统性研究，需要新的基准和评估方法。

Method: 作者引入了一个专门用于此设置的新基准，并评估了九种修订方法（包括顺序反思和并行采样变体），使用gpt-oss-20b/120b、gpt-5.4-mini和qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b等多个模型。同时探索了成本效益的测试时计算方法，其中最有效的是从三路并行样本中选择，使用基于LLM的选择或质心（medoid）选择策略。

Result: 基线方法在基准上达到了68.3%到93%的准确率。最具成本效益的方法是使用LLM选择或质心选择从三个并行样本中挑选结果，该方法将准确率提高了2.2%到9.7%。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在对话式工件修订传播中，并行采样配合有效选择策略是一种具有成本效益的改进方案。质心选择等无需额外LLM调用的方法即可取得显著准确率提升，对实际部署具有吸引力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often help users generate artifacts through iterative cycles of generation and revision in conversation. A challenge here is that, when users specify only a local change during revision, LLMs must instead identify the relevant dependencies and propagate the revision to all affected parts of the artifact. This paper studies this ability of LLMs on conversationally generated artifacts, where the artifact context and its dependencies may be embedded in the conversation history. Toward practical use, we also explore cost-effective test-time compute for this new setting. Specifically, we introduce a new benchmark for this setting, and evaluate nine revision methods, including sequential reflection and parallel sampling variants, using gpt-oss-20b/120b, gpt-5.4-mini, and qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b on the benchmark. The results show that baselines achieve accuracies of 68.3--93%, and the most cost-effective method is selecting from three parallel samples using either LLM-based or medoid selection, which improves accuracy by 2.2--9.7%. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ntt-dkiku/llm-revision-propagation.

</details>


### [130] [Contextual Tamil Spelling and Grammar Correction Using Progressively Fine-Tuned Sequence-to-Sequence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03273)
*Karthikeyan A,Jaya Nirmala S,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Indhu R,Pranav Kumar,Bharat Jude Johnson,Vishnu Ram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端序列到序列（seq2seq）方法用于泰米尔语拼写和语法纠错，通过微调mT5-small和mBART-50，在包含657,720个噪声-干净句对的合成语料上训练，并采用四阶段渐进式训练策略（分别针对表面噪声、上下文语法、单点sandhi和多点跨词sandhi）。最佳模型mBART-50 v5在1,000句平衡诊断集上达到69.3%的top-1精确匹配准确率，sandhi分类达87.5%，主谓一致达43.5%。研究还量化了sandhi召回率与身份（identity）准确率之间的单调权衡，并发现泰米尔语适配的指令模型（Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct）无法在没有任务特定监督的情况下迁移到专门的句子级纠错任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰米尔语作为黏着型低资源语言，具有丰富的动词形态、复杂的词边界sandhi音变规则以及包含247个字母的书写系统，其拼写和语法纠错极具挑战性。现有方法仅针对单词级表面错误（如规则方法、统计n-gram、最小编辑距离或带transformer重排器的混合流水线），无法处理需要句子级理解的上下文错误——主谓一致、时态一致或跨词sandhi。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出能够处理句子级上下文错误的方法。

Method: (1) 提出端到端seq2seq公式化方法； (2) 在包含657,720对噪声-干净泰米尔语句对的合成语料上微调mT5-small和mBART-50，覆盖十类错误； (3) 两个骨干模型共享相同的四阶段渐进式训练调度，每阶段针对一个弱点：v2表面噪声、v3上下文语法、v4单点sandhi、v5多点跨词sandhi； (4) 使用1,000句平衡诊断集（与训练数据不重叠）进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型mBART-50 v5达到69.3% top-1精确匹配准确率，其中sandhi为87.5%、主谓一致为43.5%。渐进式调度产生显著增益：引入上下文语料对后主谓一致准确率从1.0%升至52.5%，引入多点sandhi语料对后sandhi从0%升至87.5%。研究还首次量化了precision-recall权衡：sandhi召回率与身份准确率呈单调抵消关系。基线Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct零样本仅达19.0%，三次示范后为24.7%，而复制基线为20.0%——表明泰米尔语适配的指令模型不会自动迁移到需要任务特定监督的专门句子级纠错任务。

Conclusion: 四阶段渐进式训练调度是取得性能提升的关键因素，而非仅依赖骨干模型的选择。该研究首次系统报告了sandhi纠错中召回率与身份保持率之间的单调权衡，为后续研究提供了重要参考。同时揭示通用或泰米尔语适配的大语言模型在缺乏任务特定监督时，无法有效处理需要句子级理解的专门纠错任务，体现了task-specific训练的必要性。

Abstract: Tamil spell and grammar correction is challenging because Tamil is an agglutinative low-resource language with rich verbal morphology, complex sandhi (phonetic transformation) rules at word boundaries, and a script of 247 distinct letters. Prior work targets word-level surface errors with rule-based methods, statistical n-gram models, Minimum Edit Distance, or hybrid pipelines with a transformer re-ranker; such methods cannot reliably handle contextual errors - subject-verb agreement, tense consistency, or cross-word sandhi - which require sentence-level understanding. We propose an end-to-end sequence-to-sequence formulation and fine-tune mT5-small and mBART-50 on a synthetic corpus of up to 657,720 noisy-clean Tamil sentence pairs spanning ten error categories. Both backbones follow the same four-stage progressive schedule, each stage targeting one weakness: surface noise (v2), contextual grammar (v3), single-site sandhi (v4), and multi-site cross-word sandhi (v5). On a 1,000-sentence balanced diagnostic set verified disjoint from all training data, our best model, mBART-50 v5, reaches 69.3% top-1 exact-match accuracy, with 87.5% on sandhi and 43.5% on subject-verb agreement. The schedule is what produces these gains: subject-verb accuracy rises from 1.0% to 52.5% once contextual pairs are introduced, and sandhi from 0% to 87.5% once multi-site sandhi pairs are.
